It supports multimodal inputs (text, images,<br>audio, video, file), offers configurable parameters (like temperature,<br>max tokens, tool use), and supports both streaming and non-streaming<br>output modes.<br>parameters:<br>- $ref: '#/components/parameters/AcceptLanguage'<br>requestBody:<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>oneOf:<br>- $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatCompletionTextRequest'<br>title: Text Model<br>- $ref: '#/components/schemas/ChatCompletionVisionRequest'<br>title: Vision Model<br>examples:<br>Basic Example:<br>value:<br>model: glm-5.3<br>messages:<br>- role: system<br>content: You are a helpful coding assistant.<br>- role: user<br>content: &gt;-<br>Write a Python function to calculate the factorial of a<br>number.<br>temperature: 1<br>stream: false<br>Stream Example:<br>value:<br>model: glm-5.3<br>messages:<br>- role: system<br>content: You are a helpful coding assistant.<br>- role: user<br>content: &gt;-<br>Write a Python function to calculate the factorial of a<br>number.<br>temperature: 1<br>stream: true<br>Thinking Example:<br>value:<br>model: glm-5.3<br>messages:<br>- role: system<br>content: You are a helpful coding assistant.<br>- role: user<br>content: &gt;-<br>Write a Python function to calculate the factorial of a<br>number.<br>thinking:<br>type: enabled<br>stream: true<br>Multi Conversation:<br>value:<br>model: glm-5.3<br>messages:<br>- role: system<br>content: You are a professional programming assistant.<br>- role: user<br>content: What is recursion?<br>- role: assistant<br>content: &gt;-<br>Recursion is a programming technique where a function<br>calls itself to solve a problem... If you change the Base URL to [https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4](https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4), calls use standard API billing and **may deduct from your account balance even while plan quota remains**.<br>• Check the key configured in your tool. **Standard keys do not use Coding Plan quota. An existing personal Compatible key also uses standard API billing when calling the standard API endpoint.** To use plan quota, get a key from the corresponding Coding Plan entry point and replace the key in your tool. For setup instructions, see the [Quick Start](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/quick-start.md). |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.z.ai/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># API Key Management Guide<br>&gt; Starting October 16, 2026, API keys for the standard model API and GLM Coding Plan are managed separately. Learn which key to use, how existing keys are classified, and how billing works for each key type.<br>* Publication date: October 16, 2026<br>Starting October 16, 2026, API keys for the standard model API (pay-as-you-go or usage bundles) and GLM Coding Plan are managed separately. When creating a key, choose the entry point for the type of usage you need. Existing keys will be classified automatically; you do not need to replace them as part of this update.<br>## Which API key should I use?<br>&#124; Your needs &#124; Key type &#124; Where to get it and how it is billed &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; Pay-as-you-go usage or usage bundles &#124; Standard API Key &#124; Open [API Keys](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list) and create a key under Standard API Key. Calls use your account balance or applicable usage bundles, not your Coding Plan quota. &#124;<br>&#124; Individual Plan usage &#124; API Key &#124; Create an API Key under [API Keys → Personal Coding Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list). Calls use your Individual Plan quota. &#124;<br>&#124; Team Plan usage &#124; Team Plan Key &#124; Get your key from [Team Coding Plan → My Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/coding-plan/team/my-plan). Usage follows your Team Plan and seat rules. &#124;<br>**For Standard and Coding Plan keys created from October 16 onward, the key type determines how calls are billed.** Across supported endpoints, Standard keys use standard API billing, while Coding Plan keys use the corresponding plan. Changing the endpoint does not change the key's billing type.<br>&gt; For example, using a Standard API Key in a coding tool does not consume your Coding Plan quota, even if you have a subscription. It may incur pay-as-you-go charges against your account balance.<br>## What happens to existing API keys?<br>Keys created before October 16, 2026 are classified as follows. **Your key values remain unchanged, and this classification update does not require you to reconfigure existing keys.**<br>&#124; Existing key belongs to &#124; Classification &#124; How it works &#124;<br>&#124; :- &#124; :- &#124; :- &#124;<br>&#124; A Team Plan project &#124; Team Plan Key &#124; Follows Team Plan and seat rules. &#124;<br>&#124; An account that has ever purchased or had an active Coding Plan, outside a Team Plan project &#124; Compatible key &#124; Retains its existing usage and billing rules. See "How Compatible keys work and are billed" below. &#124;<br>&#124; An account that has never had a Coding Plan &#124; Standard key &#124; Uses standard API billing. &#124;<br>Some existing keys for dedicated enterprise services or partner channels retain their previous compatibility behavior. Refer to the key's classification in the console and your existing service agreement.<br>### How Compatible keys work and are billed<br>A **Compatible** label means your key retains its existing calling behavior and billing rules. You can continue using it as before. **Billing for an existing Compatible key depends on both the endpoint and whether the account has ever had a Coding Plan, regardless of which list the key appears in.**<br>Common endpoints and their billing behavior are listed below:<br>API / endpoint<br>Base URL configured in your tool<br>Billing for a Compatible key<br>OpenAI Chat Completions — standard API<br>[https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4](https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4)<br>Uses your account balance or applicable usage bundles. Does not consume Coding Plan quota.<br>OpenAI Chat Completions — Coding Plan endpoint<br>[https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4](https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4)<br>If the account has ever had a Coding Plan, calls use plan quota. Calls stop when the plan expires.<br>If the account has never had a Coding Plan, calls use the account balance or applicable usage bundles.<br>Anthropic Messages — general endpoint<br>[https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic](https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic)<br>OpenAI Responses — general endpoint<br>[https://api.z.ai/api/v1](https://api.z.ai/api/v1)<br>&gt; For example, if your account currently has an active Coding Plan with available quota, the same existing Compatible key uses plan quota at [https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4](https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4). If you change the Base URL to [https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4](https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4), calls use standard API billing and **may deduct from your account balance even while plan quota remains**.<br>## FAQ<br>### How does this update affect me? Do I need to reconfigure anything?<br>* **You can keep using your existing keys.** This update does not require you to replace keys or reconfigure your tools.<br>* **Choose the right entry point when creating a new key.** To use Coding Plan quota, get a key from [API Keys → Personal Coding Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list) or [Team Coding Plan → My Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/coding-plan/team/my-plan). For pay-as-you-go usage or usage bundles, create a Standard API Key on the [API Keys page](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list).<br>### Why is my account balance being charged when I have a Coding Plan?<br>Check the key configured in your tool. **Standard keys do not use Coding Plan quota. An existing personal Compatible key also uses standard API billing when calling the standard API endpoint.** To use plan quota, get a key from the corresponding Coding Plan entry point and replace the key in your tool. For setup instructions, see the [Quick Start](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/quick-start.md).<br>### How do I switch an existing Compatible key to pay-as-you-go after my plan expires?<br>After your plan expires, calls made with an existing Compatible key to plan endpoints do not automatically switch to pay-as-you-go billing. To continue:<br>1. Open the [API Keys page](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list) and create a Standard API Key.<br>2. Replace the Compatible key in your tool or application with the new Standard key. **You can keep the same endpoint.**<br>Make sure your account has an available balance or applicable usage bundles. Subsequent calls use standard API billing instead of Coding Plan quota.<br>If on-demand usage overage is enabled for your Team Plan, it continues to follow the existing settings and billing rules.<br>### Why can't I find the API Key entry for my Individual Plan?<br>The **Personal Coding Plan** tab on the [API Keys page](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list) appears for accounts that have subscribed to an Individual Plan. If it is missing, make sure you are signed in to the account used for that subscription. Team Plan members should get their keys from [Team Coding Plan → My Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/coding-plan/team/my-plan).<br>***<br>If your key's classification or API responses do not match the expected behavior, contact [Technical Support](https://z.ai/contact) with the error details and request ID. Keep your API keys secure and never share a complete key.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

If you change the Base URL to [https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4](https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4), calls use standard API billing and **may deduct from your account balance even while plan quota remains**.</li><li>* **Choose the right entry point when creating a new key.** To use Coding Plan quota, get a key from [API Keys → Personal Coding Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list) or [Team Coding Plan → My Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/coding-plan/team/my-plan). For pay-as-you-go usage or usage bundles, create a Standard API Key on the [API Keys page](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list).</li><li>### Why is my account balance being charged when I have a Coding Plan?</li><li>Check the key configured in your tool. **Standard keys do not use Coding Plan quota. Team Plan members should get their keys from [Team Coding Plan → My Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/coding-plan/team/my-plan).</li></ul></details> |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.z.ai/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Quick Start<br>&gt; [!TIP]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; This guide will help you get started with [GLM Coding Plan](https://z.ai/subscribe?utm_source=zai\&amp;utm_medium=link\&amp;utm_term=quickstart\&amp;utm_campaign=Platform_Ops&amp;_channel_track_key=DRUfXN42) in minutes—from subscribing to using GLM models in officially [supported tools and products](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/others.md#step-1-supported-tools).<br>## Getting Started<br>**1. Register or Login**<br>* Access [Z.AI Open Platform](https://z.ai/model-api), Register or Login.<br>**2. Subscribe to GLM Coding Plan**<br>After logging in, navigate to the [GLM Coding Plan](https://z.ai/subscribe?utm_source=zai\&amp;utm_medium=link\&amp;utm_term=quickstart\&amp;utm_campaign=Platform_Ops&amp;_channel_track_key=DRUfXN42) to select your preferred subscription plan.<br>**3. Obtain API Key**<br>After subscribing,<br>Individual Plan users (including trial card recipients) can create an API Key under [Personal Coding Plan &gt; Plan Overview](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/apikey-list).<br>Team Plan members can create an API Key under [Team Coding Plan &gt; My Plan](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/coding-plan/team/my-plan).<br>&gt; [!TIP]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; API Keys for Individual Plans, Team Plans, and the standard model API are now managed separately. Newly created API Keys for these three product types are not interchangeable—use the one that matches your needs. For details, see the [API Key Management Guide](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/notice/api-key-management.md).<br>&gt; [!WARNING]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Safeguard your API Key by keeping it confidential and avoiding hard-coding it in your code.<br>**4. Connect a Tool**<br>The GLM Coding Plan is strictly limited to use within officially [supported tools and products](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/others.md#step-1-supported-tools). Click a tool below to open its configuration guide:<br>&gt; [card] [Claude Code](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/claude)<br>&gt; [card] [Roo Code](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/roo)<br>&gt; [card] [Kilo Code](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/kilo)<br>&gt; [card] [Cline](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/cline)<br>&gt; [card] [OpenCode](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/opencode)<br>&gt; [card] [OpenClaw](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/openclaw)<br>&gt; [card] [Crush](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/crush)<br>&gt; [card] [Goose](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/goose)<br>&gt; [card] [Cursor](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/cursor)<br>&gt; [card] [Other Tools](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/others.md)<br>**5. Endpoint Guide**<br>GLM Coding Plan supports both the Anthropic and OpenAI protocols. Make sure to configure the correct `Base URL`:<br>&#124; Protocol &#124; Base URL &#124;<br>&#124; - &#124; - &#124;<br>&#124; Anthropic Messages &#124; `https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic` &#124;<br>&#124; OpenAI Chat Completions &#124; `https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4` &#124;<br>&#124; OpenAI Responses &#124; `https://api.z.ai/api/v1` &#124;<br>**6. Start Coding**<br>Once configured, you can begin coding with the GLM model!<br>&gt; [tab] Conversational Programming<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={null}<br>&gt; # Using natural language commands in Claude Code<br>&gt; Please create a React component containing a user login form<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] Code Debugging<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={null}<br>&gt; # Describe the issue encountered<br>&gt; My API request returns a 404 error. Please help me check the code.<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] Code Optimization<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={null}<br>&gt; # Code optimization<br>&gt; This function performs poorly. Please optimize it for me.<br>&gt; ```<br>## Advanced Features<br>&gt; [accordion] Vision MCP Server (Coding Plan Exclusive)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; All users can utilize the Vision MCP Server, which employs the flagship vision reasoning model to comprehend and analyze image content.<br>&gt;<br>&gt; * Analyze UI design mockups and generate corresponding code<br>&gt; * Understand flowcharts and architecture diagrams<br>&gt; * Extract text and information from screenshots<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For detailed usage instructions, refer to the [Vision MCP Server](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/mcp/vision-mcp-server.md) documentation.<br>&gt; [accordion] Web Search MCP Server (Coding Plan Exclusive)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; All users can utilize the Web Search MCP Server to access the latest technical information.<br>&gt;<br>&gt; * Search for the latest technical documentation and API changes<br>&gt; * Obtain the latest information on open-source projects<br>&gt; * Find solutions and best practices<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For detailed usage instructions, refer to the [Web Search MCP Server](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/mcp/search-mcp-server.md) documentation.<br>&gt; [accordion] Web Reader MCP Server (Coding Plan Exclusive)<br>&gt;<br>&gt; All users can utilize the Web Reader MCP Server to fetch full webpage content and extract structured data.<br>&gt;<br>&gt; * Fetch complete webpage content including text, and links<br>&gt; * Extract structured data such as title, body, and metadata<br>&gt; * Remote HTTP-based MCP service, no local installation required<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For detailed usage instructions, refer to the [Web Reader MCP Server](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/mcp/reader-mcp-server.md) documentation.<br>This documentation is built and hosted on [Mintlify](https://mintlify.com), a developer documentation platform. |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • &gt; Cross-source intelligent retrieval and information synthesis, delivering structured research reports fast—so every decision is backed by evidence.<br>• * **Access to High-Intelligence Models:** Upon release, the GLM series achieved SOTA performance among open-source models in reasoning, coding, and agent capabilities, delivering outstanding results in tool use and complex task execution.<br>• * **Expanded Capabilities:** All plans support Vision Understanding, Web Search MCP， Web Reader MCP and Zread MCP helping you tackle a wider range of coding and office tasks.<br>• &gt; You can view the number of tokens consumed under each pricing type and the number of tool calls on the [Charge Type](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/billing) page.<br>• &gt; [card] [Vision Understanding](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/mcp/vision-mcp-server.md) |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.z.ai/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Overview<br>The GLM Coding Plan is your AI partner for tasks and code — a single subscription that unlocks a suite of agent tools for AI-powered coding and everyday office work.<br>## Usage<br>The plan can be applied to agent tools such as ZCode, Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, AutoClaw, and OpenClaw, covering both coding and office scenarios:<br>### Coding<br>&gt; [accordion] Natural Language Programming<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Describe requirements in plain language to automatically generate plans, write code, debug issues, and ensure smooth execution.<br>&gt; [accordion] Intelligent Code Completion<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Get real-time, context-aware completion suggestions that reduce manual typing and significantly improve productivity.<br>&gt; [accordion] Code Debugging &amp; Repair<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Input error messages or descriptions to automatically analyze your codebase, locate problems, and provide fixes.<br>&gt; [accordion] Codebase Q\&amp;A<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Ask questions about your team’s codebase anytime, maintain global understanding, and receive precise answers with external data integration.<br>&gt; [accordion] Automated Task Handling<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Automatically fix lint issues, resolve merge conflicts, and generate release notes—allowing developers to stay focused on core logic.<br>### Office Work<br>&gt; [accordion] Deep Search &amp; Research<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Cross-source intelligent retrieval and information synthesis, delivering structured research reports fast—so every decision is backed by evidence.<br>&gt; [accordion] Business Data Analysis<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Upload multi-dimensional business data to automatically generate visual charts, along with in-depth business insights and operational recommendations.<br>&gt; [accordion] Creative Ideation &amp; Brainstorming<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Break through mental boundaries and spark inspiration from multiple angles, turning vague ideas into actionable plans — fast.<br>&gt; [accordion] Automated Task Execution<br>&gt;<br>&gt; Autonomously run complex workflows end to end, from instruction to delivery — cutting repetitive work and freeing up time for what matters.<br>## Advantages<br>* **Access to High-Intelligence Models:** Upon release, the GLM series achieved SOTA performance among open-source models in reasoning, coding, and agent capabilities, delivering outstanding results in tool use and complex task execution.<br>* **Works with 20+ Agent Tools:** Beyond Claude Code, it also supports ZCode, Cline, OpenCode, AutoClaw, and other [supported tools](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/tool/others.md#step-1-supported-tools), giving you flexibility across both coding and office workflows.<br>* **Generous Usage at a Fair Price:** Get higher call limits than standard plans. Starting at just 18 USD per month, with Pro and Max plans designed for high-frequency, complex projects.<br>* **Expanded Capabilities:** All plans support Vision Understanding, Web Search MCP， Web Reader MCP and Zread MCP helping you tackle a wider range of coding and office tasks.<br>## Benefits<br>### Supported Models<br>* All plans support **GLM-5.3**, GLM-5.3-Flash.<br>* Requests for GLM-5.2/GLM-5.1 will be automatically routed to GLM-5.3, requests for GLM-4.7 will automatically be routed to GLM-5.3-Flash.<br>### Usage Instruction<br>&gt; [!WARNING]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; For information on Team Plan usage limits, please visit [Team Plan Benefits](https://docs.z.ai/devpack/teamplan.md).<br>#### Usage Credit Allowance<br>Each plan is subject to both a 5-hour usage limit and a weekly usage limit.<br>&#124; Plan Type &#124; 5-Hour Credits &#124; Weekly Credits &#124;<br>&#124; :-: &#124; :-: &#124; :-: &#124;<br>&#124; Lite &#124; 2,000 &#124; 10,000 &#124;<br>&#124; Pro &#124; 12,000 &#124; 60,000 &#124;<br>&#124; Max &#124; 28,000 &#124; 140,000 &#124;<br>**Credit Reset Rules**<br>* **5-hour credits**: Dynamically refreshed; credit quota resets 5 hours after consumption.<br>* **Weekly credits**: Activated upon subscription; resets every 7 days.<br>#### Credit Calculation<br>* Model credit usage = (Input tokens × Input multiplier + Cached Input tokens × Cached Input multiplier + Output tokens × Output multiplier) / 10,000<br>* MCP tool credit usage = Number of calls × Output multiplier<br>&gt; [!TIP]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; You can view the number of tokens consumed under each pricing type and the number of tool calls on the [Charge Type](https://z.ai/manage-apikey/billing) page.<br>Product Type<br>Product<br>Input Multiplier<br>Cached Input Multiplier<br>Output Multiplier<br>Model<br>GLM-5.3<br>6.9<br>1.7<br>24<br>GLM-5.3-Flash\<br>(Including MCP for visual understanding)<br>2.3<br>0.56<br>8<br>MCP Server<br>Web Search<br>—<br>—<br>1.2<br>Web Reader<br>—<br>—<br>1.2<br>Zread<br>—<br>—<br>1.2<br>**During off-peak hours, model usage is charged at 50% of the standard credit rate**.<br>&gt; [!NOTE]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; **Peak hours**: Monday to Friday, 14:00–18:00 Singapore Standard Time (UTC+8).<br>#### Estimated Token Allowance<br>Token usage varies depending on the cache hit rate, as shown below:<br>&#124; Cache Hit Rate &#124; Model &#124; Lite (M Tokens/week) &#124; Pro (M Tokens/week) &#124; Max (M Tokens/week) &#124; |

It allows users<br>to upload reference materials that can enhance translation accuracy and<br>consistency.<br>requestBody:<br>content:<br>multipart/form-data:<br>schema:<br>type: object<br>properties:<br>purpose:<br>type: string<br>description: Upload purpose<br>default: user_data<br>enum:<br>- user_data<br>- agent<br>file:<br>type: string<br>format: binary<br>description: &gt;-<br>File to upload. `agent` limit to `100MB`, `user_data` limit<br>to `1T`. `agent` allowed formats: `pdf`, `doc`, `xlsx`,<br>`ppt`, `txt`, `jpg`, `png`, `user_data` allowed formats:<br>`pptx`, `ppt`, `docx`, `doc`, `xlsx`, `xls`, `pdf`.<br>required:<br>- purpose<br>- file<br>required: true<br>responses:<br>'200':<br>description: Processing successful<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>type: object<br>properties:<br>id:<br>type: string<br>description: Unique identifier of the uploaded file.<br>object:<br>type: string<br>description: Object type.<br>bytes:<br>type: integer<br>description: File size in bytes.<br>filename:<br>type: string<br>description: Name of the uploaded file.<br>purpose:<br>type: string<br>description: Purpose of the uploaded file.<br>created_at:<br>type: integer<br>description: Timestamp of file creation.<br>default:<br>description: The request has failed.<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/Error'<br>components:<br>schemas:<br>Error:<br>required:<br>- code<br>- message<br>type: object |

Only supports `GLM-Image` model.<br>requestBody:<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/AsyncCreateImageRequest'<br>examples:<br>Image Generation Example:<br>value:<br>model: glm-image<br>prompt: &gt;-<br>A cute little kitten sitting on a sunny windowsill, with the<br>background of blue sky and white clouds.<br>size: 1280x1280<br>required: true<br>responses:<br>'200':<br>description: Processing successful<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/AsyncResponse'<br>default:<br>description: The request has failed.<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/Error'<br>components:<br>schemas:<br>AsyncCreateImageRequest:<br>type: object<br>required:<br>- model<br>- prompt<br>properties:<br>model:<br>type: string<br>description: Model code<br>enum:<br>- glm-image<br>example: glm-image<br>prompt:<br>type: string<br>description: The text description of the image to be generated.<br>example: A cute little kitten.<br>quality:<br>type: string<br>description: &gt;-<br>The quality of the generated image. `hd`: Generates a more detailed<br>and rich image with higher overall consistency, takes about `20`<br>seconds.<br>enum:<br>- hd<br>default: hd<br>size:<br>type: string<br>description: &gt;-<br>Image size, recommended enum values: `1280x1280` (default),<br>`1568x1056`, `1056x1568`, `1472x1088`, `1088x1472`, `1728x960`,<br>`960x1728`.<br>Custom parameter: Both width and height must be between<br>`1024px-2048px`, and must be divisible by `32`, and the maximum<br>pixel count must not exceed `2^22px`.<br>default: 1280x1280<br>example: 1280x1280<br>user_id:<br>type: string |

Supported audio file formats:</li><li>`.wav / .mp3`. Specifications: file size ≤ `25 MB`, audio duration ≤</li><li>Base64 encoded audio file. Only one of file_base64 or file needs to</li><li>In long text scenarios, you can provide previous transcription</li><li>AudioTranscriptionResponse:</li><li>description: The complete transcribed content of the audio.</li><li>AudioTranscriptionStreamResponse:</li><li>Audio transcription event type. `transcript.text.delta` indicates</li><li>transcription in progress, `transcript.text.done` indicates</li><li>transcription completed.</li><li>description: Incremental audio transcription information returned by the model.</li></ul></details> |

Use the [GLM-ASR-2512](/guides/audio/glm-asr-2512) model to transcribe audio files into text, supporting multiple languages and real-time streaming transcription.<br>## OpenAPI<br>````yaml POST /paas/v4/audio/transcriptions<br>openapi: 3.0.1<br>info:<br>title: Z.AI API<br>description: Z.AI API available endpoints<br>license:<br>name: Z.AI Developer Agreement and Policy<br>url: https://chat.z.ai/legal-agreement/terms-of-service<br>version: 1.0.0<br>contact:<br>name: Z.AI Developers<br>url: https://chat.z.ai/legal-agreement/privacy-policy<br>email: user_feedback@z.ai<br>servers:<br>- url: https://api.z.ai/api<br>description: Production server<br>security:<br>- bearerAuth: []<br>paths:<br>/paas/v4/audio/transcriptions:<br>post:<br>summary: Speech to Text<br>description: &gt;-<br>Use the [GLM-ASR-2512](/guides/audio/glm-asr-2512) model to transcribe<br>audio files into text, supporting multiple languages and real-time<br>streaming transcription.<br>requestBody:<br>content:<br>multipart/form-data:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/AudioTranscriptionRequest'<br>example:<br>model: glm-asr-2512<br>stream: false<br>required: true<br>responses:<br>'200':<br>description: Request processed successfully<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/AudioTranscriptionResponse'<br>text/event-stream:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/AudioTranscriptionStreamResponse'<br>default:<br>description: Request failed.<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/Error'<br>components:<br>schemas:<br>AudioTranscriptionRequest:<br>type: object<br>required:<br>- file<br>- model<br>properties:<br>file:<br>type: string<br>format: binary<br>description: &gt;-<br>The audio file to be transcribed. Supported audio file formats:<br>`.wav / .mp3`. Specifications: file size ≤ `25 MB`, audio duration ≤<br>`30 seconds`.<br>file_base64:<br>type: string<br>description: &gt;-<br>Base64 encoded audio file. Only one of file_base64 or file needs to<br>be provided (if both are provided, file takes precedence).<br>model:<br>type: string<br>description: The model ID to invoke.<br>enum:<br>- glm-asr-2512<br>default: glm-asr-2512<br>prompt:<br>type: string<br>description: &gt;-<br>In long text scenarios, you can provide previous transcription<br>results as context. Recommended to be less than 8000 characters.<br>hotwords:<br>type: array<br>description: &gt;-<br>Hotword list to improve recognition accuracy for domain-specific<br>vocabulary. Format example: ["person_name","place_name"].<br>Recommended not to exceed 100 items.<br>items:<br>type: string<br>maxItems: 100<br>stream:<br>type: boolean<br>default: false |

| capability mentions | 新增 | 未记录 | • # Structured Output<br>• Structured output (JSON mode) ensures that AI returns JSON data conforming to predefined formats, providing reliable guarantees for programmatic processing of AI outputs.<br>• The structured output feature provides AI models with strict data format control capabilities, supporting various complex data structures and validation requirements.<br>• * **`model`**: Use models that support structured output, such as `glm-5`, `glm-4.7`, `glm-4.6`, etc.<br>• The following is a complete structured output example demonstrating how to perform sentiment analysis and return structured JSON results:<br>• &gt;                     Please extract structured information from product description, return in the following format:<br>• &gt;                     - Identify content type (article, video, image, document, etc.) |
| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.z.ai/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Structured Output<br>Structured output (JSON mode) ensures that AI returns JSON data conforming to predefined formats, providing reliable guarantees for programmatic processing of AI outputs.<br>## Features<br>The structured output feature provides AI models with strict data format control capabilities, supporting various complex data structures and validation requirements.<br>### Core Parameters<br>* **`response_format`**: Specifies the response format, set to `{"type": "json_object"}` to enable JSON mode<br>* **`model`**: Use models that support structured output, such as `glm-5`, `glm-4.7`, `glm-4.6`, etc.<br>* **`messages`**: Define the expected JSON structure and field requirements in system messages<br>## Code Examples<br>**Install SDK**<br>```bash theme={null}<br># Install latest version<br>pip install zai-sdk<br># Or specify version<br>pip install zai-sdk==0.2.3<br>```<br>**Verify Installation**<br>```python theme={null}<br>import zai<br>print(zai.__version__)<br>```<br>**Complete Example**<br>The following is a complete structured output example demonstrating how to perform sentiment analysis and return structured JSON results:<br>```python theme={null}<br>from zai import ZaiClient<br>import json<br># Initialize client<br>client = ZaiClient(api_key="your-api-key")<br># Basic JSON mode<br>response = client.chat.completions.create(<br>model="glm-5",<br>messages=[<br>{<br>"role": "system",<br>"content": """<br>You are a sentiment analysis expert. Please return analysis results in the following JSON format:<br>{<br>"sentiment": "positive/negative/neutral",<br>"confidence": 0.95,<br>"emotions": ["joy", "excitement"],<br>"keywords": ["weather", "mood"],<br>"analysis": "Detailed analysis explanation"<br>}<br>"""<br>},<br>{<br>"role": "user",<br>"content": "Please analyze the sentiment of this sentence: 'The weather is really nice today, I'm feeling very happy!'"<br>}<br>],<br>response_format={<br>"type": "json_object"<br>}<br>)<br># Parse results<br>result = json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)<br>print(f"Sentiment: {result['sentiment']}")<br>print(f"Confidence: {result['confidence']}")<br>print(f"Emotions: {result['emotions']}")<br>```<br>## Basic Usage<br>&gt; [tab] Simple JSON Output<br>&gt;<br>&gt; **Simple JSON Output**<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={null}<br>&gt; from zai import ZaiClient<br>&gt;<br>&gt; client = ZaiClient(api_key="your-api-key")<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Basic JSON mode<br>&gt; response = client.chat.completions.create(<br>&gt;     model="glm-5",<br>&gt;     messages=[<br>&gt;         {<br>&gt;             "role": "user",<br>&gt;             "content": "Please analyze the sentiment of this sentence: 'The weather is really nice today, I'm feeling very happy!'"<br>&gt;         }<br>&gt;     ],<br>&gt;     response_format={<br>&gt;         "type": "json_object"<br>&gt;     }<br>&gt; )<br>&gt;<br>&gt; import json<br>&gt; result = json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)<br>&gt; print(result)<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt; [tab] Specify JSON Structure<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ### Specify JSON Structure<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={null}<br>&gt; # Specify specific JSON structure<br>&gt; response = client.chat.completions.create(<br>&gt;     model="glm-5",<br>&gt;     messages=[ |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.z.ai/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Tokenizer<br>&gt; `Tokenizer` is used to split text into `tokens` recognizable by the model and calculate the count. It receives user input text, processes it through the model for tokenization, and finally returns the corresponding `token` count. It is suitable for text length evaluation, model input estimation, dialogue context truncation, cost calculation, etc.<br>## OpenAPI<br>````yaml POST /paas/v4/tokenizer<br>openapi: 3.0.1<br>info:<br>title: Z.AI API<br>description: Z.AI API available endpoints<br>license:<br>name: Z.AI Developer Agreement and Policy<br>url: https://chat.z.ai/legal-agreement/terms-of-service<br>version: 1.0.0<br>contact:<br>name: Z.AI Developers<br>url: https://chat.z.ai/legal-agreement/privacy-policy<br>email: user_feedback@z.ai<br>servers:<br>- url: https://api.z.ai/api<br>description: Production server<br>security:<br>- bearerAuth: []<br>paths:<br>/paas/v4/tokenizer:<br>post:<br>summary: Text Tokenizer<br>description: &gt;-<br>`Tokenizer` is used to split text into `tokens` recognizable by the<br>model and calculate the count. It receives user input text, processes it<br>through the model for tokenization, and finally returns the<br>corresponding `token` count. It is suitable for text length evaluation,<br>model input estimation, dialogue context truncation, cost calculation,<br>etc.<br>requestBody:<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/TokenizerRequest'<br>examples:<br>Text Tokenization Example:<br>value:<br>model: glm-4.6<br>messages:<br>- role: user<br>content: &gt;-<br>What opportunities and challenges will the Chinese large<br>model industry face in 2025?<br>required: true<br>responses:<br>'200':<br>description: Business processing successful<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/TokenizerResponse'<br>default:<br>description: The request has failed.<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/Error'<br>components:<br>schemas:<br>TokenizerRequest:<br>type: object<br>required:<br>- model<br>- messages<br>properties:<br>model:<br>type: string<br>description: The model code to be called.<br>example: glm-4.6<br>default: glm-4.6<br>enum:<br>- glm-4.6<br>messages:<br>type: array<br>description: &gt;-<br>The current conversation message list as the model’s prompt input,<br>provided in JSON array format, e.g.,`{“role”: “user”, “content”:<br>“Hello”}`. Possible message types include system messages, user<br>messages. Note: The input must not consist of system or assistant<br>messages only.<br>items:<br>oneOf:<br>- title: User Message<br>type: object<br>properties:<br>role:<br>type: string<br>enum:<br>- user<br>description: Role of the message author<br>default: user<br>content:<br>oneOf:<br>- type: array |

| excerpt | 新增 | 未记录 | &gt; ## Documentation Index<br>&gt; Fetch the complete documentation index at: https://docs.z.ai/llms.txt<br>&gt; Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br># Function Calling<br>Function Calling allows AI models to call external functions and APIs, greatly expanding the capability boundaries of intelligent agents, enabling them to perform specific operations and obtain real-time data.<br>## Features<br>Function calling provides AI models with the ability to interact with external systems, supporting various complex application scenarios and integration requirements.<br>### Core Parameter Description<br>* **`tools`**: Defines the list of callable functions, including function names, descriptions, and parameter specifications<br>* **`tool_choice`**: Controls function calling strategy, default is `auto` (only supports `auto`)<br>### Response Parameter Description<br>Key fields in function calling responses:<br>* **`tool_calls`**: Contains information about functions the model decides to call<br>* **`function.name`**: Name of the called function<br>* **`function.arguments`**: Function call parameters (JSON format string)<br>* **`id`**: Unique identifier for the tool call<br>## Code Examples<br>By defining function tools and handling function calls, AI models can perform various external operations:<br>&gt; [tab] Python SDK<br>&gt;<br>&gt; **Install SDK**<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```bash theme={null}<br>&gt; # Install latest version<br>&gt; pip install zai-sdk<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Or specify version<br>&gt; pip install zai-sdk==0.2.3<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt;<br>&gt; **Verify Installation**<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={null}<br>&gt; import zai<br>&gt; print(zai.__version__)<br>&gt; ```<br>&gt;<br>&gt; **Complete Example**<br>&gt;<br>&gt; ```python theme={null}<br>&gt; import json<br>&gt; from zai import ZaiClient<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Initialize client<br>&gt; client = ZaiClient(api_key='your_api_key')<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Define weather query function<br>&gt; def get_weather(city: str) -&gt; dict:<br>&gt;     """Get weather information for specified city"""<br>&gt;     # This should call a real weather API<br>&gt;     weather_data = {<br>&gt;         "city": city,<br>&gt;         "temperature": "22°C",<br>&gt;         "condition": "Sunny",<br>&gt;         "humidity": "65%",<br>&gt;         "wind_speed": "5 km/h"<br>&gt;     }<br>&gt;     return weather_data<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Define function tools<br>&gt; tools = [<br>&gt;     {<br>&gt;         "type": "function",<br>&gt;         "function": {<br>&gt;             "name": "get_weather",<br>&gt;             "description": "Get current weather information for specified city",<br>&gt;             "parameters": {<br>&gt;                 "type": "object",<br>&gt;                 "properties": {<br>&gt;                     "city": {<br>&gt;                         "type": "string",<br>&gt;                         "description": "City name, e.g.: Beijing, Shanghai"<br>&gt;                     }<br>&gt;                 },<br>&gt;                 "required": ["city"]<br>&gt;             }<br>&gt;         }<br>&gt;     }<br>&gt; ]<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Make conversation request<br>&gt; response = client.chat.completions.create(<br>&gt;     model="glm-5.3",  # Use model that supports function calling<br>&gt;     messages=[<br>&gt;         {"role": "user", "content": "How's the weather in Beijing today?"}<br>&gt;     ],<br>&gt;     tools=tools,         # Pass function tools<br>&gt;     tool_choice="auto"   # Automatically choose whether to call functions<br>&gt; )<br>&gt;<br>&gt; # Handle function calls<br>&gt; message = response.choices[0].message<br>&gt; messages = [{"role": "user", "content": "How's the weather in Beijing today?"}]<br>&gt; messages.append(message.model_dump())<br>&gt;<br>&gt; if message.tool_calls:<br>&gt;     for tool_call in message.tool_calls:<br>&gt;         if tool_call.function.name == "get_weather":<br>&gt;             # Parse parameters and call function<br>&gt;             args = json.loads(tool_call.function.arguments) |

Building upon traditional search engine capabilities like web crawling and ranking, it enhances intent recognition to return results better suited for LLM processing (including webpage titles, URLs, summaries, site names, favicons etc.).<br>## OpenAPI<br>````yaml POST /paas/v4/web_search<br>openapi: 3.0.1<br>info:<br>title: Z.AI API<br>description: Z.AI API available endpoints<br>license:<br>name: Z.AI Developer Agreement and Policy<br>url: https://chat.z.ai/legal-agreement/terms-of-service<br>version: 1.0.0<br>contact:<br>name: Z.AI Developers<br>url: https://chat.z.ai/legal-agreement/privacy-policy<br>email: user_feedback@z.ai<br>servers:<br>- url: https://api.z.ai/api<br>description: Production server<br>security:<br>- bearerAuth: []<br>paths:<br>/paas/v4/web_search:<br>post:<br>description: &gt;-<br>The [Web Search](/guides/tools/web-search) is a specialized search<br>engine for large language models. Building upon traditional search<br>engine capabilities like web crawling and ranking, it enhances intent<br>recognition to return results better suited for LLM processing<br>(including webpage titles, URLs, summaries, site names, favicons etc.).<br>parameters:<br>- $ref: '#/components/parameters/AcceptLanguage'<br>requestBody:<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/WebSearchRequest'<br>required: true<br>responses:<br>'200':<br>description: Processing successful<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/WebSearchResponse'<br>default:<br>description: The request has failed.<br>content:<br>application/json:<br>schema:<br>$ref: '#/components/schemas/Error'<br>components:<br>parameters:<br>AcceptLanguage:<br>name: Accept-Language<br>in: header<br>schema:<br>type: string<br>description: Config desired response language for HTTP requests.<br>default: en-US,en<br>example: en-US,en<br>enum:<br>- en-US,en<br>required: false<br>schemas:<br>WebSearchRequest:<br>type: object<br>properties:<br>search_engine:<br>type: string<br>description: &#124;-<br>The search engine code to call.<br>search-prime: Z.AI Premium Version Search Engine<br>example: search-prime<br>default: search-prime<br>enum:<br>- search-prime<br>search_query:<br>type: string<br>description: The content to be searched.<br>count:<br>type: integer<br>description: &#124;-<br>The number of results to return<br>Fillable range: `1-50`, maximum `50` results per single search<br>Default is `10`<br>Supported search engines:<br>`search_pro_jina`.<br>minimum: 1<br>maximum: 50<br>search_domain_filter:<br>type: string<br>description: &gt;-<br>Used to limit the scope of search results and only return content<br>from specified whitelist domains.<br>Whitelist: Directly enter the domain name (e.g., `www.example.com`)<br>Supported search engines: |

## zhipu 专属证据与核对说明

### 智谱 / Z.AI IR 观察候选

- IR 指纹：`sha256:69a0a6b7befa14391d26d2eb83374abbf2fc35a7c736f65db2370fd3d318a34a`
- 来源快照：54 份
- 中国目录 ID：37 个
- 国际目录 ID：10 个
- 当前输出为来源观察 IR；尚无字段投影到 Provider 服务事实。

#### 待人工核对

- 模型目录、价格、API 参数和服务能力按中国与国际来源分别保存；它们尚未按 Service/Offering/Route/PriceFact 契约完成映射。
